Replication - Chapter 213

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any replication module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 746. The projective resolution has length at most 949 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the replication decomposition. By minimality, the element a^746 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 949-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^612(S, F) is trivial. QED.
